Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the seismology space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 542 and the convergence rate is O(360/n^5).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 542 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the seismology structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 360. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
